FILE: .\src\providers\AuthProvider.tsx

import { Session, User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';

export type AppRole =
  | 'member'
  | 'leader'
  | 'mentor'
  | 'pastor'
  | 'local_admin'
  | 'system_admin';

export type MembershipState =
  | 'loading'
  | 'ready'
  | 'pending'
  | 'rejected'
  | 'none';

export type Profile = {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  first_name?: string | null;
  middle_name?: string | null;
  surname?: string | null;
  suffix?: string | null;
  mobile: string | null;
  sex?: 'male' | 'female' | null;
  username?: string | null;
  login_method?: string | null;
  login_identifier?: string | null;
  avatar_url: string | null;
  church_id: string | null;
  is_verified?: boolean;
  connection_type?: 'local_member'|'invited'|'seeking_church'|'community'|null;
  trust_level?: number|null;

  // Legacy primary role.
  // Do not use this as the sole authorization source.
  role: AppRole;

  is_active: boolean;
};

type AuthContextValue = {
  session: Session | null;
  user: User | null;
  profile: Profile | null;

  // Active simultaneous BUKLOD roles.
  roles: AppRole[];

  loading: boolean;
  membershipState: MembershipState;

  hasRole: (role: AppRole) => boolean;
  hasAnyRole: (rolesToCheck: AppRole[]) => boolean;

  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshRoles: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshMembership: () => Promise<void>;

  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
};

const VALID_ROLES: AppRole[] = [
  'member',
  'leader',
  'mentor',
  'pastor',
  'local_admin',
  'system_admin',
];

const LEADERSHIP_ROLES: AppRole[] = [
  'leader',
  'mentor',
  'pastor',
  'local_admin',
  'system_admin',
];

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<AppRole[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [membershipState, setMembershipState] =
    useState<MembershipState>('loading');

  const loadRoles = async (
    userId?: string,
    legacyRole?: AppRole | null
  ): Promise<AppRole[]> => {
    if (!userId) {
      setRoles([]);
      return [];
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_roles')
      .select('role')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('is_active', true);

    if (error) {
      console.warn('Role load failed:', error.message);

      // Compatibility fallback only if user_roles could not be read.
      const fallback: AppRole[] =
        legacyRole === 'system_admin'
          ? ['system_admin']
          : ['member'];

      if (
        legacyRole &&
        VALID_ROLES.includes(legacyRole) &&
        !fallback.includes(legacyRole)
      ) {
        fallback.push(legacyRole);
      }

      setRoles(fallback);
      return fallback;
    }

    const nextRoles = Array.from(
      new Set(
        (data ?? [])
          .map((row: any) => row.role as AppRole)
          .filter((role: AppRole) => VALID_ROLES.includes(role))
      )
    );

    // Every active BUKLOD account has Member as its baseline role.
    if (
      !nextRoles.includes('system_admin') &&
      !nextRoles.includes('member')
    ) {
      nextRoles.unshift('member');
    }

    setRoles(nextRoles);

    return nextRoles;
  };

  const loadMembership = async (
    userId?: string,
    _activeRoles: AppRole[] = []
  ) => {
    if (!userId) {
      setMembershipState('none');
      return;
    }

    // Verification is now the universal entry gate.
    // Role and group do not bypass System Admin verification.
    const { data: verification, error: verificationError } =
      await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('is_verified')
        .eq('id', userId)
        .maybeSingle();

    if (verificationError) {
      console.warn(
        'Verification load failed:',
        verificationError.message
      );
      setMembershipState('pending');
      return;
    }

    if (verification?.is_verified === true) {
      setMembershipState('ready');
      return;
    }

    const { data: request } = await supabase
      .from('church_join_requests')
      .select('status')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (request?.status === 'rejected') {
      setMembershipState('rejected');
      return;
    }

    // Signed-in but not System Admin verified:
    // keep the account on the onboarding/verification screen.
    setMembershipState('pending');
  };

  const loadProfile = async (userId?: string) => {
    if (!userId) {
      setProfile(null);
      setRoles([]);
      setMembershipState('none');
      return;
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select(
        'id,full_name,first_name,middle_name,surname,suffix,mobile,sex,username,login_method,login_identifier,avatar_url,church_id,connection_type,trust_level,is_verified,role,is_active'
      )
      .eq('id', userId)
      .single();

    if (error) {
      console.warn('Profile load failed:', error.message);
      setProfile(null);
      setRoles([]);
      setMembershipState('none');
      return;
    }

    const nextProfile = data as Profile;

    setProfile(nextProfile);

    const nextRoles = await loadRoles(
      userId,
      nextProfile.role
    );

    await loadMembership(userId, nextRoles);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(async ({ data }) => {
      setSession(data.session);

      await loadProfile(data.session?.user.id);

      setLoading(false);
    });

    const { data: listener } =
      supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(
        async (_event, nextSession) => {
          /* PATCH_171C_SIGNIN_DIRECT_HOME_RACE_FIX */
          if (_event === 'SIGNED_IN') {
            // Initial app boot already starts with loading=true.
            // A manual sign-in happens after loading has become false,
            // so raise the gate again until loadProfile() finishes.
            setLoading(true);
          }

          setSession(nextSession);

          await loadProfile(nextSession?.user.id);

          setLoading(false);
        }
      );

    return () => {
      listener.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(() => {
    const hasRole = (role: AppRole) =>
      roles.includes(role);

    const hasAnyRole = (
      rolesToCheck: AppRole[]
    ) =>
      rolesToCheck.some((role) =>
        roles.includes(role)
      );

    return {
      session,
      user: session?.user ?? null,
      profile,
      roles,
      loading,
      membershipState,

      hasRole,
      hasAnyRole,

      refreshProfile: async () => {
        await loadProfile(session?.user.id);
      },

      refreshRoles: async () => {
        const nextRoles = await loadRoles(
          session?.user.id,
          profile?.role
        );

        await loadMembership(
          session?.user.id,
          nextRoles
        );
      },

      refreshMembership: async () => {
        await loadMembership(
          session?.user.id,
          roles
        );
      },

      signOut: async () => {
        await supabase.auth.signOut();
      },
    };
  }, [
    session,
    profile,
    roles,
    loading,
    membershipState,
  ]);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const value = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(
      'useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider'
    );
  }

  return value;
}

FILE: .\src\hooks\useBuklodAccess.ts
====================================================
import {
  useCallback,
  useMemo,
} from 'react';

import { useAuth } from '@/providers/AuthProvider';
import { useBuklodScope } from '@/providers/BuklodScopeProvider';

import type { BuklodEntitlement } from '@/lib/buklodAccess';
import {
  isScopeAdmin,
  normalizedScopeRoles,
  scopeHasEntitlement,
  scopeHasRole,
  verifyScopeEntitlement,
} from '@/lib/buklodAccess';

export function useBuklodAccess() {
  const { hasRole: hasAppRole } = useAuth();

  const {
    activeScope,
    loading,
  } = useBuklodScope();

  const systemAdmin =
    hasAppRole('system_admin');

  const roleKeys =
    useMemo(
      () => normalizedScopeRoles(activeScope),
      [activeScope]
    );

  const hasRole =
    useCallback(
      (role: string) =>
        systemAdmin ||
        scopeHasRole(activeScope, role),
      [activeScope, systemAdmin]
    );

  const hasEntitlement =
    useCallback(
      (entitlement: BuklodEntitlement) =>
        systemAdmin ||
        scopeHasEntitlement(
          activeScope,
          entitlement
        ),
      [activeScope, systemAdmin]
    );

  const verifyEntitlement =
    useCallback(
      async (
        entitlement: BuklodEntitlement
      ) => {
        if (systemAdmin) return true;

        return verifyScopeEntitlement(
          activeScope,
          entitlement
        );
      },
      [activeScope, systemAdmin]
    );

  return {
    loading,
    activeScope,
    scopeType:
      activeScope?.scope_type ?? null,
    roleKeys,
    isSystemAdmin:
      systemAdmin,
    isAdmin:
      systemAdmin ||
      isScopeAdmin(activeScope),
    hasRole,
    hasEntitlement,
    verifyEntitlement,
  };
}

FILE: .\src\lib\buklodAccess.ts
====================================================
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import type { BuklodScope } from '@/lib/buklodScope';

export type BuklodEntitlement =
  | 'bayanihan'
  | 'tithes'
  | 'learn_paid'
  | 'financial_admin';

const FEATURE_ALIASES: Record<BuklodEntitlement, string[]> = {
  bayanihan: [
    'restricted.bayanihan',
    'bayanihan',
    'feature.bayanihan',
  ],
  tithes: [
    'restricted.tithes',
    'tithes',
    'giving',
    'feature.tithes',
  ],
  learn_paid: [
    'restricted.learn',
    'restricted.learn_paid',
    'learn_paid',
    'paid_learning',
  ],
  financial_admin: [
    'restricted.financial_admin',
    'financial_admin',
    'finance.admin',
  ],
};

function normalize(value: string) {
  return value.trim().toLowerCase().replaceAll('-', '_');
}

export function normalizedScopeRoles(scope: BuklodScope | null) {
  return (scope?.role_keys ?? []).map(normalize);
}

export function scopeHasRole(
  scope: BuklodScope | null,
  role: string
) {
  return normalizedScopeRoles(scope).includes(normalize(role));
}

export function isScopeAdmin(scope: BuklodScope | null) {
  if (!scope) return false;
  if (scope.is_admin) return true;

  const roles = normalizedScopeRoles(scope);

  return [
    'admin',
    'cluster_admin',
    'church_admin',
    'org_admin',
    'organization_admin',
    'system_admin',
  ].some((role) => roles.includes(role));
}

export function scopeHasEntitlement(
  scope: BuklodScope | null,
  entitlement: BuklodEntitlement
) {
  if (!scope) return false;

  const available = new Set(
    (scope.feature_keys ?? []).map(normalize)
  );

  return FEATURE_ALIASES[entitlement].some(
    (key) => available.has(normalize(key))
  );
}

function rpcResultToBoolean(data: any) {
  if (typeof data === 'boolean') return data;

  if (Array.isArray(data)) {
    return rpcResultToBoolean(data[0]);
  }

  if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
    const value =
      data.allowed ??
      data.can_access ??
      data.has_access ??
      data.enabled ??
      data.result;

    if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
      return value;
    }
  }

  return Boolean(data);
}

export async function verifyScopeEntitlement(
  scope: BuklodScope | null,
  entitlement: BuklodEntitlement
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!scope) return false;

  const featureKey =
    FEATURE_ALIASES[entitlement][0];

  const candidates = [
    {
      p_scope_id: scope.scope_id,
      p_feature_key: featureKey,
    },
    {
      _scope_id: scope.scope_id,
      _feature_key: featureKey,
    },
    {
      scope_id: scope.scope_id,
      feature_key: featureKey,
    },
  ];

  let lastError: any = null;

  for (const args of candidates) {
    const { data, error } =
      await (supabase as any).rpc(
        'buklod_can_access_feature',
        args
      );

    if (!error) {
      return rpcResultToBoolean(data);
    }

    lastError = error;

    const message =
      String(error?.message ?? '').toLowerCase();

    const signatureProblem =
      message.includes('schema cache') ||
      message.includes('could not find the function') ||
      message.includes('pgrst202') ||
      message.includes('pgrst203');

    if (!signatureProblem) break;
  }

  console.warn(
    'BUKLOD entitlement verification:',
    lastError
  );

  return false;
}

FILE: .\src\app\profile\buklod-faculty.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { MaterialCommunityIcons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { router } from 'expo-router';

import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';

type Classroom = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  subject_code: string | null;
  section_name: string | null;
  school_year: string | null;
  term: string | null;
  status: string;
};

type DashboardStats = {
  classrooms: number;
  students: number;
  pendingGrades: number;
  openAttendance: number;
};

const EMPTY_STATS: DashboardStats = {
  classrooms: 0,
  students: 0,
  pendingGrades: 0,
  openAttendance: 0,
};

export default function BuklodFacultyScreen() {
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState<Classroom[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DashboardStats>(EMPTY_STATS);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [section, setSection] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const activeClasses = useMemo(
    () => classes.filter(item => item.status === 'ACTIVE'),
    [classes]
  );

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data: auth } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    const uid = auth.user?.id;
    if (!uid) return;

    const { data: classroomRows, error } = await supabase
      .from('buklod_education_classrooms')
      .select('id,name,subject_code,section_name,school_year,term,status')
      .eq('faculty_user_id', uid)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (error) {
      Alert.alert('BUKLOD Faculty', error.message);
      return;
    }

    const rows = (classroomRows ?? []) as Classroom[];
    setClasses(rows);

    const ids = rows.map(item => item.id);
    let students = 0;
    let pendingGrades = 0;
    let openAttendance = 0;

    if (ids.length > 0) {
      const [enrollmentRes, gradesRes, attendanceRes] = await Promise.all([
        supabase
          .from('buklod_education_enrollments')
          .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
          .in('classroom_id', ids)
          .eq('status', 'ACTIVE'),
        supabase
          .from('buklod_education_grades')
          .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
          .in('classroom_id', ids)
          .in('status', ['DRAFT', 'COMPUTED', 'REVIEWED']),
        supabase
          .from('buklod_education_attendance_sessions')
          .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
          .in('classroom_id', ids)
          .is('closed_at', null),
      ]);

      students = enrollmentRes.count ?? 0;
      pendingGrades = gradesRes.count ?? 0;
      openAttendance = attendanceRes.count ?? 0;
    }

    setStats({
      classrooms: rows.filter(item => item.status === 'ACTIVE').length,
      students,
      pendingGrades,
      openAttendance,
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await load();
    setRefreshing(false);
  };

  const createClass = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      return Alert.alert('Create Class', 'Enter a class/subject name.');
    }

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const { data: auth } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const uid = auth.user?.id;
      if (!uid) throw new Error('No signed-in BUKLOD user.');

      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('buklod_education_classrooms')
        .insert({
          faculty_user_id: uid,
          name: name.trim(),
          subject_code: code.trim() || null,
          section_name: section.trim() || null,
          status: 'ACTIVE',
        });

      if (error) throw error;

      setName('');
      setCode('');
      setSection('');
      setShowCreate(false);
      await load();
    } catch (error: any) {
      Alert.alert('Create Class', String(error?.message ?? error));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <ScrollView
        style={styles.screen}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={refresh} />}
      >
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View>
            <Text style={styles.eyebrow}>ACADEMIC OPERATIONS</Text>
            <Text style={styles.title}>BUKLOD Faculty</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              Teach more. Let BUKLOD handle routine academic operations.
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.statsGrid}>
          {[
            ['Active Classes', stats.classrooms],
            ['Active Students', stats.students],
            ['Grades to Review', stats.pendingGrades],
            ['Open Attendance', stats.openAttendance],
          ].map(([label, value]) => (
            <View key={String(label)} style={styles.statCard}>
              <Text style={styles.statValue}>{String(value)}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{String(label)}</Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>My Classes</Text>
          <Pressable style={styles.primarySmall} onPress={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
            <MaterialCommunityIcons name="plus" size={19} color="#fff" />
            <Text style={styles.primarySmallText}>Create</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        {activeClasses.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No active class yet</Text>
            <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
              Create a class. Students, materials, attendance, assessments, and grades
              will be tied to that classroom.
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          activeClasses.map(item => (
            <Pressable
              key={item.id}
              style={styles.classCard}
              onPress={() =>
                router.push({
                  pathname: '/profile/buklod-faculty-class' as any,
                  params: { id: item.id, name: item.name },
                })
              }
            >
              <View style={styles.classIcon}>
                <MaterialCommunityIcons name="google-classroom" size={26} color="#1f7a3f" />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.classTitle}>{item.name}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.classMeta}>
                  {[item.subject_code, item.section_name].filter(Boolean).join(' â€¢ ') || 'Active classroom'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </Pressable>
          ))
        )}

        <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Faculty Autopilot</Text>

        {[
          ['account-group-outline', 'Roster & Groups', 'Enroll students, form groups, and track individual contribution.'],
          ['folder-multiple-outline', 'Materials', 'Share PDF, PPT, Word, video, and downloadable files.'],
          ['calendar-check-outline', 'Attendance', 'Time in/out, location/QR verification, and exception handling.'],
          ['clipboard-text-outline', 'Assessments', 'Quizzes, exams, paper scans, rubrics, and review queues.'],
          ['chart-box-outline', 'Gradebook', 'Automatic totals, manual entries, weighted categories, audit trail.'],
          ['account-star-outline', 'Participation & Conduct', 'Recitation, engagement, group work, and rubric-based professional conduct.'],
          ['chart-timeline-variant', 'Progress & Reports', 'Current standing, final grades, consultation, and Excel-ready reports.'],
          ['alert-circle-outline', 'Exception Queue', 'Review only uncertain grades, anomalies, and student cases needing attention.'],
        ].map(([icon, title, desc]) => (
          <View key={String(title)} style={styles.toolCard}>
            <MaterialCommunityIcons name={icon as any} size={24} color="#1f7a3f" />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={styles.toolTitle}>{title}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.toolText}>{desc}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>

      <Modal visible={showCreate} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
        <View style={styles.modalShade}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Create Class</Text>

            <Text style={styles.label}>Class / Subject Name</Text>
            <TextInput value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder="e.g. Community Health Nursing II" style={styles.input} />

            <Text style={styles.label}>Subject Code</Text>
            <TextInput value={code} onChangeText={setCode} placeholder="e.g. CHN 2" style={styles.input} />

            <Text style={styles.label}>Section</Text>
            <TextInput value={section} onChangeText={setSection} placeholder="e.g. BSN 3A" style={styles.input} />

            <View style={styles.modalActions}>
              <Pressable style={styles.secondary} onPress={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
                <Text style={styles.secondaryText}>Cancel</Text>
              </Pressable>
              <Pressable disabled={busy} style={styles.primary} onPress={createClass}>
                <Text style={styles.primaryText}>{busy ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Class'}</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  screen: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f4f7f2' },
  content: { padding: 20, paddingTop: 48, paddingBottom: 60 },
  header: { marginBottom: 20 },
  eyebrow: { color: '#1f7a3f', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.4 },
  title: { color: '#17311f', fontSize: 30, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 },
  subtitle: { color: '#667169', marginTop: 5, lineHeight: 19, maxWidth: 520 },
  statsGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10, marginBottom: 24 },
  statCard: { width: '48%', backgroundColor: '#fff', borderRadius: 17, padding: 15 },
  statValue: { fontSize: 26, fontWeight: '900', color: '#17311f' },
  statLabel: { color: '#6a756d', marginTop: 2, fontWeight: '700' },
  sectionHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 10 },
  sectionTitle: { color: '#17311f', fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '900', marginBottom: 10, marginTop: 4 },
  primarySmall: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, backgroundColor: '#1f7a3f', borderRadius: 12, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 8 },
  primarySmallText: { color: '#fff', fontWeight: '900' },
  empty: { backgroundColor: '#fff', borderRadius: 18, padding: 18, marginBottom: 22 },
  emptyTitle: { color: '#22352a', fontWeight: '900', fontSize: 16 },

-- BUKLOD EDUCATION PHASE 1
-- Faculty + Classroom shared backend foundation.
-- Safe principle: students can read only their own enrollment/records;
-- grade writes are never granted to students.

create table if not exists public.buklod_education_classrooms (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  faculty_user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete restrict,
  name text not null,
  subject_code text,
  section_name text,
  school_year text,
  term text,
  status text not null default 'ACTIVE'

-- Classroom visibility: faculty owner OR enrolled student.
drop policy if exists buklod_edu_classroom_read on public.buklod_education_classrooms;
create policy buklod_edu_classroom_read
on public.buklod_education_classrooms
for select
to authenticated
using (
  faculty_user_id = auth.uid()
  or exists (
    select 1
    from public.buklod_education_enrollments e
    where e.classroom_id = id
      and e.student_user_id = auth.uid()

create or replace function public.buklod_education_validate_category_weights(p_classroom_id uuid)
returns boolean
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
  select coalesce(sum(weight_percent),0) <= 100.0001
  from public.buklod_education_grade_categories
  where classroom_id = p_classroom_id;
$$;

on public.buklod_education_attendance_log
for insert to authenticated
with check (
  student_user_id = auth.uid()
  and exists (
    select 1
    from public.buklod_education_attendance_sessions s
    join public.buklod_education_enrollments e
      on e.classroom_id = s.classroom_id

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\migrations\20260817125500_buklod_education_phase3_functional.sql
LINE: 15

drop policy if exists buklod_edu_attendance_log_student_insert on public.buklod_education_attendance_log;
create policy buklod_edu_attendance_log_student_insert
on public.buklod_education_attendance_log
for insert to authenticated
with check (
  student_user_id = auth.uid()
  and exists (
    select 1
    from public.buklod_education_attendance_sessions s
    join public.buklod_education_enrollments e
      on e.classroom_id = s.classroom_id
    where s.id = session_id
      and s.closed_at is null
      and e.student_user_id = auth.uid()
      and e.status = 'ACTIVE'
  )
);

create index if not exists buklod_education_grade_items_category_idx
  on public.buklod_education_grade_items(category_id);

-- Backfill category_id only when an existing legacy text category clearly matches
-- a category name in the same classroom. Unmatched historical rows remain nullable.
update public.buklod_education_grade_items gi
set category_id = gc.id
from public.buklod_education_grade_categories gc
where gi.category_id is null
  and gc.classroom_id = gi.classroom_id
  and lower(trim(gc.name)) = lower(trim(coalesce(gi.category, '')));

create or replace function public.buklod_education_roster_v1(p_classroom_id uuid)
returns table(
  id uuid,
  student_user_id uuid,
  status text,
----------------------------------------------------

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\migrations\20260817132000_buklod_education_phase4_productivity.sql
LINE: 38
-- Backfill category_id only when an existing legacy text category clearly matches
-- a category name in the same classroom. Unmatched historical rows remain nullable.
update public.buklod_education_grade_items gi
set category_id = gc.id
from public.buklod_education_grade_categories gc

declare
  v_grade public.buklod_education_grades%rowtype;
begin
  select g.*
  into v_grade
  from public.buklod_education_grades g
  join public.buklod_education_classrooms c on c.id = g.classroom_id
  where g.id = p_grade_id
    and c.faculty_user_id = auth.uid();

  if v_grade.id is null then raise exception 'Grade not found or not authorized.'; end if;
  if v_grade.status <> 'RELEASED' then raise exception 'Only released grades may be locked.'; end if;

  update public.buklod_education_grades
  set status = 'LOCKED', updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()

    grade_id, changed_by, old_score, new_score, old_status, new_status, reason

  v_result uuid;
  v_final numeric;
begin
  select ga.*
  into v_activity
  from public.buklod_education_group_activities ga
  join public.buklod_education_classrooms c on c.id = ga.classroom_id
  where ga.id = p_activity_id
    and c.faculty_user_id = auth.uid();

  if v_activity.id is null then raise exception 'Activity not found or not authorized.'; end if;

  if p_group_score < 0 or p_group_score > v_activity.points_possible
     or p_individual_score < 0 or p_individual_score > v_activity.points_possible then
    raise exception 'Scores must be within the activity point range.';
  end if;

      (p_group_score * v_activity.group_weight_percent / 100.0)

  category_scores as (
    select
      r.student_user_id,
      gc.id as category_id,

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\migrations\20260817140500_buklod_education_phase5_materials_gradeflow.sql

    from roster r
    cross join public.buklod_education_grade_categories gc
    left join public.buklod_education_grade_items gi

    from category_scores

      on g.grade_item_id = gi.id
     and g.student_user_id = r.student_user_id
    where gc.classroom_id = p_classroom_id
    group by r.student_user_id, gc.id, gc.weight_percent
  ),
  totals as (
    select
      student_user_id,
      round(sum(
        case when possible > 0 then (earned / possible) * weight_percent else 0 end
      ), 4) as current_weighted_grade,
      sum(released_count)::bigint as released_components
    from category_scores
    group by student_user_id
  )
  select

  totals as (

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\migrations\20260817143000_buklod_education_phase6_assessment_engine.sql

    a.total_points,

  where a.classroom_id = p_classroom_id
    and a.status = 'PUBLISHED'
    and (a.available_from is null or a.available_from <= now())
    and (a.available_until is null or a.available_until >= now())
    and exists(select 1 from allowed)
  order by a.created_at desc;

-- BUKLOD EDUCATION PHASE 7
-- Assessment -> gradebook integration.
-- Faculty selects the category/grade item.
-- Student assessment actions never write grades directly.
-- Only faculty release/sync paths create or change grade records.

alter table public.buklod_education_assessments
  add column if not exists grade_item_id uuid null
    references public.buklod_education_grade_items(id) on delete set null,
  add column if not exists sync_to_gradebook boolean not null default false;

create index if not exists buklod_education_assessment_grade_item_idx
  on public.buklod_education_assessments(grade_item_id);

create index if not exists buklod_education_grade_item_assessment_idx

  from public.buklod_education_assessment_attempts a
  join public.buklod_education_assessments x

      from public.buklod_education_classrooms c
      where c.id = a.classroom_id
        and c.faculty_user_id = auth.uid()
    );

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Only a faculty-owned GRADED attempt may be released.';
  end if;

  select
    coalesce(x.sync_to_gradebook,false) and x.grade_item_id is not null
  into v_should_sync
  from public.buklod_education_assessment_attempts a
  join public.buklod_education_assessments x

  where a.id = p_attempt_id;

    perform public.buklod_education_sync_assessment_attempt_grade_v1(

      'Assessment released by faculty'

-- 4. Account activation/deactivation. System admin only.

create or replace function public.set_user_active(
  p_user_id uuid,
  p_active boolean
)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not public.is_system_admin() then
    raise exception 'Only the system administrator can change account status';
  end if;
----------------------------------------------------

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\05_admin_cms.sql
LINE: 276
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not public.is_system_admin() then
    raise exception 'Only the system administrator can change account status';
  end if;

  if p_user_id = (select auth.uid()) and p_active = false then
    raise exception 'You cannot deactivate your own administrator profile';
  end if;

  update public.profiles
  set is_active = p_active
  where id = p_user_id;

  insert into public.audit_logs (actor_id, action, entity_type, entity_id, metadata)

  );

  new_id uuid;

create or replace function public.can_manage_event(p_event_id uuid)
returns boolean
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select exists (
----------------------------------------------------

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\09_church_life.sql
LINE: 232
  select exists (
    select 1
    from public.church_events e
    where e.id = p_event_id
      and (
        public.is_system_admin()
        or (
          e.scope = 'church'
          and public.current_role() in ('pastor','local_admin')
          and e.church_id = public.my_church_id()
        )
        or (
          e.scope = 'group'
          and public.can_manage_group(e.group_id)
        )
      )
  );
$$;

FILE: C:\Users\Jose Solis\Downloads\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\BUKLOD_Production_Starter_v16\supabase\09_church_life.sql

  select exists (

    from public.ministries m

        or (
          public.current_role() in ('pastor','local_admin')
          and m.church_id = public.my_church_id()
        )
      )
  );
$$;

create policy "System admins create system events"
on public.church_events for insert to authenticated
with check (
  organizer_id = (select auth.uid())

    public.is_system_admin()

      and church_id = public.my_church_id()

      scope = 'group'
      and public.can_manage_group(group_id)

security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not public.is_system_admin() then
    raise exception 'System administrator access required';
  end if;

  return query
  select
    c.id,

declare
  r public.content_reports%rowtype;
begin
  if not public.is_system_admin() then
    raise exception 'System administrator access required';
  end if;

  select * into r
  from public.content_reports
  where id=p_report_id
  for update;

    raise exception 'Report not found';
  end if;

    is_hidden=false

          select 1
          from public.group_members gm
          join public.buklod_groups g on g.id=gm.group_id
          where gm.user_id=prayer_requests.user_id

  cfg public.app_runtime_config%rowtype;
  pilot_ok boolean := true;

  features jsonb;

  select * into cfg
  from public.app_runtime_config
  where environment=p_environment;

    select * into cfg
    from public.app_runtime_config

security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select case p_audience
    when 'all' then true
    when 'member' then public.current_role() in ('member','leader','pastor','local_admin','system_admin')
    when 'leader' then public.current_role() in ('leader','pastor','local_admin','system_admin')
    when 'pastoral' then public.current_role() in ('pastor','local_admin','system_admin')
    when 'admin' then public.current_role() in ('local_admin','system_admin')
    else false
  end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.help_audience_allowed(text) from public;
grant execute on function public.help_audience_allowed(text) to authenticated;

-- 01_schema.sql

create extension if not exists pgcrypto;
do $$ begin
  create type public.app_role as enum ('member','leader','pastor','local_admin','system_admin');
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;
do $$ begin
  create type public.content_privacy as enum ('private','leader','group','church');
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;
do $$ begin
  create type public.follow_up_status as enum ('open','contacted','awaiting_response','completed');
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;
do $$ begin
  create type public.prayer_status as enum ('active','answered','archived');
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;
create table if not exists public.churches (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),

      and exists (

      )

      join public.group_members theirs on theirs.group_id = mine.group_id

grant execute on function public.my_church_id() to authenticated;

grant execute on function public.can_manage_group(uuid) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.can_view_profile(uuid) to authenticated;
